<text x="1432" y="1684" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#286c68">收沖提液</text>
<text x="1421" y="1734" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">改條件，藥進收集液</text>
<text x="70" y="1826" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#286c68">逆相：常以甲醇潤濕後水／緩衝平衡；沖提提高相容有機比例或改形態。pH、容量、流速與藥的穩定性都要合。</text>
<text x="70" y="1940" font-size="29" font-weight="600" fill="#286c68">C｜離子交換：名字是抓誰，表面固定電荷相反</text>
<text x="70" y="1983" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">分析物的解離、弱交換基本身的電荷、基材耐受是三份不同判斷；鹽也要認真正對離子。</text>
<rect x="65" y="2045" width="850" height="390" rx="18" fill="#e8e1f2" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="87" y="2087" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#685185">陽離子交換抓正電</text>
<polyline points="135,2138 385,2138" fill="none" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<circle cx="160" cy="2190" r="27" fill="#fff" stroke="#685185"/>
<text x="151" y="2198" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#685185">−</text>
<circle cx="219" cy="2190" r="29" fill="#fff1c8" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<text x="198" y="2198" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#9e771c">BH⁺</text>
<circle cx="290" cy="2190" r="27" fill="#fff" stroke="#685185"/>
<text x="281" y="2198" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#685185">−</text>
<circle cx="349" cy="2190" r="29" fill="#fff1c8" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<text x="328" y="2198" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#9e771c">BH⁺</text>
<text x="450" y="2165" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#685185">固定表面 ↔ 目標電荷</text>
<text x="87" y="2283" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">NaCl中的Na⁺可作陽交換對離子。提高競爭鹽或讓弱鹼變B等可沖提；弱交</text>
<text x="87" y="2317.08" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">換基自身仍需帶負電。</text>
<rect x="970" y="2045" width="850" height="390" rx="18" fill="#e8e1f2" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="992" y="2087" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#685185">陰離子交換抓負電</text>
<polyline points="1040,2138 1290,2138" fill="none" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<circle cx="1065" cy="2190" r="27" fill="#fff" stroke="#685185"/>
<text x="1056" y="2198" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#685185">+</text>
<circle cx="1124" cy="2190" r="29" fill="#fff1c8" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<text x="1103" y="2198" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#9e771c">A⁻</text>
<circle cx="1195" cy="2190" r="27" fill="#fff" stroke="#685185"/>
<text x="1186" y="2198" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#685185">+</text>
<circle cx="1254" cy="2190" r="29" fill="#fff1c8" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<text x="1233" y="2198" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#9e771c">A⁻</text>
<text x="1355" y="2165" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#685185">固定表面 ↔ 目標電荷</text>
<text x="992" y="2283" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">NaCl中的Cl⁻是陰交換對離子。提高競爭鹽或讓弱酸變HA等可沖提；弱交換</text>
<text x="992" y="2317.08" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">基本身電荷另判。</text>
<text x="70" y="2490" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#685185">OH⁻不能靠「把弱酸變中性」解釋沖提：高pH更偏A⁻，釋放可能是競爭或弱交換基失電，須依實際表面判。</text>
<text x="70" y="2605" font-size="29" font-weight="600" fill="#286c68">D｜選擇把柄：RAM的內外表面；硼酸親和的局部二醇</text>
<text x="70" y="2648" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">左圖是限進原理；右圖只畫鄰位二醇局部例，不虛構HbA1c或藥物完整結構／產物。</text>
<circle cx="360" cy="2830" r="134" fill="#d9eeea" stroke="#286c68"/>
<circle cx="360" cy="2830" r="82" fill="#e8e1f2" stroke="#685185"/>
<text x="84" y="2734" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#286c68">親水外部／限進</text>
<polyline points="250,2727 302,2740" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="307" y="2862" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#685185">內部作用</text>
<circle cx="99" cy="2830" r="40" fill="#c4ccd1" stroke="#61717a"/>
<polyline points="145,2830 217,2830" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="217.0,2830.0 205.6,2836.2 205.6,2823.8" fill="#61717a"/>
<text x="65" y="2908" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">大分子通常限入</text>
<circle cx="631" cy="2800" r="12" fill="#685185" stroke="#685185"/>
<polyline points="610,2800 429,2800" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="429.0,2800.0 440.4,2793.8 440.4,2806.2" fill="#61717a"/>
<text x="570" y="2893" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#685185">可進入的小分子</text>
<text x="70" y="2990" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#286c68">限進＋減外部蛋白吸附＋內部作用；不是「只看大小」，也不是所有小分子都保</text>
<text x="70" y="3022.66" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#286c68">留。</text>
<polyline points="1199.8801085141083,2889.5 1128.0,2931.0 1056.1198914858917,2889.5 1056.1198914858917,2806.5 1128.0,2765.0 1199.8801085141083,2806.5 1199.8801085141083,2889.5" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polyline points="1187.6604900667098,2882.445 1128.0,2916.89" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polyline points="1068.3395099332902,2882.445 1068.3395099332902,2813.555" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polyline points="1128.0,2779.11 1187.6604900667098,2813.555" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polyline points="1128.0,2765.0 1128.0,2726.82" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="1111.0" y="2719.82" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">OH</text>
<polyline points="1199.8801085141083,2806.5 1232.944958430598,2787.41" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="1215.944958430598" y="2780.41" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">OH</text>
<text x="990" y="2977" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#685185">Catechol：鄰位OH例</text>
<rect x="1333" y="2735" width="485" height="285" rx="18" fill="#e8e1f2" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1355" y="2776" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#685185">硼酸親和把柄</text>
<text x="1355" y="2824.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">可接近且幾何適當的二醇，在相容pH下可</text>
<text x="1355" y="2857" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">逆結合。HbA1c是糖化血紅蛋白；結果需依</text>
<text x="1355" y="2889.66" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">方法校正，不是所有兩OH都可。</text>
<text x="70" y="3120" font-size="29" font-weight="600" fill="#286c68">E｜微透析與SPME：都需匹配取樣條件，出口不同</text>
<text x="70" y="3163" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">左：灌流液沿膜腔流動，游離小分子由外側擴散進膜；右：HS與DI是兩種選擇，不是串接步驟。</text>
<rect x="70" y="3240" width="817" height="364" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="91" y="3283" font-size="26" font-weight="600" fill="#326991">微透析：半透膜管腔示意</text>
<rect x="170" y="3374" width="500" height="75" rx="35" fill="#fff" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<polyline points="97,3411 752,3411" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="752.0,3411.0 740.6,3417.2 740.6,3404.8" fill="#326991"/>
<text x="91" y="3490" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#326991">零目標入口</text>
<text x="660" y="3490" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#326991">透析液 → 分時收集</text>
<circle cx="310" cy="3317" r="10" fill="#685185" stroke="#685185"/>
<polyline points="310,3332 310,3389" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="310.0,3389.0 303.8,3377.6 316.2,3377.6" fill="#61717a"/>
<circle cx="460" cy="3538" r="10" fill="#685185" stroke="#685185"/>
<polyline points="460,3525 460,3431" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="460.0,3431.0 466.2,3442.4 453.8,3442.4" fill="#61717a"/>
<circle cx="585" cy="3317" r="26" fill="#c4ccd1" stroke="#61717a"/>
<text x="615" y="3324" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">蛋白通常受阻</text>
<text x="93" y="3580" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#326991">RR匹配後，c游離=c透析/RR；不是組織總量。</text>
<text x="987" y="3291" font-size="26" font-weight="600" fill="#685185">HS：頂空</text>
<path d="M 1015,3340 L 1015,3557 L 1290,3557 L 1290,3340" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3"/>
<rect x="1018" y="3450" width="269" height="103" rx="0" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<polyline points="1145,3316 1145,3402" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polyline points="1145,3385 1145,3428" fill="none" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="12" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<circle cx="1175" cy="3419" r="7" fill="#685185" stroke="#685185"/>
<circle cx="1110" cy="3398" r="7" fill="#685185" stroke="#685185"/>
<text x="1000" y="3605" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#685185">塗層在氣相</text>
<text x="1427" y="3291" font-size="26" font-weight="600" fill="#685185">DI：直接浸入</text>
<path d="M 1455,3340 L 1455,3557 L 1730,3557 L 1730,3340" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3"/>
<rect x="1458" y="3450" width="269" height="103" rx="0" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<polyline points="1585,3316 1585,3493" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polyline points="1585,3481 1585,3527" fill="none" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="12" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<circle cx="1615" cy="3519" r="7" fill="#685185" stroke="#685185"/>
<circle cx="1550" cy="3498" r="7" fill="#685185" stroke="#685185"/>
<text x="1440" y="3605" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#685185">塗層浸液相</text>
<rect x="70" y="3663" width="1758" height="178" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="92" y="3704" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#326991">兩者都不能把取到的量直接當全部</text>
<text x="92" y="3752.34" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">微透析需膜、流速、RR與時間基準；植入探針不是零侵入。SPME常非耗盡：PDMS例以分配／吸收，另有吸附塗層；之後可接GC熱脫附或LC溶劑脫附，再以匹配</text>
<text x="92" y="3786.42" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">標準量測。</text>
<text x="70" y="3950" font-size="29" font-weight="600" fill="#286c68">F｜SFE：高壓流體溶解基質成分 → 減壓捕集 → 後測</text>
<text x="70" y="3993" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">與SFC管柱分離任務不同。圖示含改質劑的配置例；不是每個SFE方法都需要改質劑。</text>
<rect x="79" y="4200" width="249" height="237" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="101" y="4241" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#326991">CO₂供液</text>
<text x="101" y="4289.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">相態與供液條件符</text>
<text x="101" y="4322" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">合方法。</text>
<rect x="415" y="4200" width="249" height="237" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="437" y="4241" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#326991">專用幫浦</text>
<text x="437" y="4289.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">形成所需流量／壓</text>
<text x="437" y="4322" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">力。</text>
<rect x="768" y="4200" width="300" height="237" rx="18" fill="#fff1c8" stroke="#9e771c" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="790" y="4241" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#9e771c">控溫萃取槽</text>
<circle cx="803" cy="4282" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="803" cy="4317" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="803" cy="4352" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="848" cy="4282" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="848" cy="4317" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="848" cy="4352" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="893" cy="4282" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="893" cy="4317" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="893" cy="4352" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="938" cy="4282" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="938" cy="4317" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="938" cy="4352" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="983" cy="4282" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="983" cy="4317" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="983" cy="4352" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="1028" cy="4282" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="1028" cy="4317" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<circle cx="1028" cy="4352" r="10" fill="#e5d9af" stroke="#9e771c"/>
<text x="790" y="4410" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#9e771c">含目標的固體基質</text>
<rect x="1172" y="4200" width="270" height="237" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1194" y="4241" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#326991">受控減壓</text>
<text x="1194" y="4289.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">背壓調節／限流器，</text>
<text x="1194" y="4322" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">調P與釋放。</text>
<rect x="1570" y="4200" width="260" height="343" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1590" y="4241" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#326991">捕集／收集</text>
<path d="M 1621,4300 L 1621,4500 L 1781,4500 L 1781,4300" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3"/>
<rect x="1625" y="4441" width="152" height="55" rx="0" fill="#e8e1f2" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1640" y="4474" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#685185">相容捕集液</text>
<polyline points="328,4343 415,4343" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="415.0,4343.0 403.6,4349.2 403.6,4336.8" fill="#326991"/>
<polyline points="664,4343 768,4343" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="768.0,4343.0 756.6,4349.2 756.6,4336.8" fill="#326991"/>
<polyline points="1068,4343 1172,4343" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="1172.0,4343.0 1160.6,4349.2 1160.6,4336.8" fill="#326991"/>
<polyline points="1442,4343 1530,4343 1530,4280 1660,4280 1660,4432" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="1660.0,4432.0 1653.8,4420.6 1666.2,4420.6" fill="#326991"/>
<rect x="415" y="4041" width="410" height="144" rx="18" fill="#fff" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="437" y="4082" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#326991">可選：改質劑幫浦</text>
<text x="437" y="4130.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">相容甲醇／乙醇等；比例依方法。</text>
<polyline points="680,4185 716,4185 716,4343" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="716.0,4343.0 709.8,4331.6 722.2,4331.6" fill="#326991"/>
<polyline points="1760,4320 1760,4280 1862,4280 1862,4098 1740,4098" fill="none" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="1740.0,4098.0 1751.4,4091.8 1751.4,4104.2" fill="#326991"/>
<text x="1430" y="4111" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#326991">CO₂氣體出口</text>
<polyline points="1700,4543 1700,4620 1419,4620" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="1419.0,4620.0 1430.4,4613.8 1430.4,4626.2" fill="#61717a"/>
<text x="92" y="4512" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#326991">藍線僅表示本圖配置的流體路徑；T、P、溶解、傳質和捕集共同決定回收。</text>
<rect x="79" y="4578" width="1340" height="206" rx="18" fill="#eef6ed" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="101" y="4619" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#356844">交M03：收集液的相容後測與量還原</text>
<text x="101" y="4667.34" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">捕集液另做HPLC、GC或其他匹配分析；收集體積與DF記錄清楚。較低溫與較少氧接觸可利穩定，但不能保證零降解、零捕</text>
<text x="101" y="4701.42" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">集損失。</text>
<rect x="79" y="4828" width="1751" height="178" rx="18" fill="#f5dfe2" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="101" y="4869" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#b72d4a">M04判定不合格，才回查具體段</text>
<text x="101" y="4917.34" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">若低回收：查LLE是否收錯相、SPE是否穿透／洗失、SFE捕集是否不全、微透析RR或SPME採樣是否失配。先定位證據，再改條件與校正。</text>
<text x="50" y="5072" font-size="20" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">本圖為自製原理示意，不是題本原圖。沒有未提供的完整藥物反應式，也不把相似裝置當成相同量測基準。</text>
<text x="50" y="5108" font-size="20" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">原題：CH23 PDF（內題頭CH24）；正文：建構講義 v3.0。M＝大循環站；L＝理解模組；P＝方法條目。示意不按比例。</text>
</svg>

</details>

### 圖5｜公式與誤差判讀總表

^ext-fig-5

獨立檔：[[藥物分析_萃取法_公式與誤差判讀總表_v3_0.svg]]

<details>
<summary>展開圖5</summary>

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1900" height="3430" viewBox="0 0 1900 3430" role="img" aria-labelledby="ext3_g5_title ext3_g5_desc" font-family="Noto Sans CJK TC, Microsoft JhengHei, Droid Sans Fallback, sans-serif" fill="#24343f">
<title id="ext3_g5_title">萃取法｜公式與誤差判讀總表</title><desc id="ext3_g5_desc">萃取法教學向量圖，v3.0。四站角色固定：先定問題、準備與測量、讀訊號、核對結論。灰線表示分析資訊；圖4藍線表示流體，灰箭頭的個體擴散另依標籤辨識。座標曲線依各子圖標示。紅虛線表示條件不符的分析者回查。示意不按比例。</desc>
<rect x="0" y="0" width="1900" height="3430" rx="0" fill="#f8f6ee" stroke="#f8f6ee" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="50" y="64" font-size="20" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">PHARMACEUTICAL ANALYSIS / CH23 / EXTRACTION / v3.0</text>
<text x="50" y="120" font-size="38" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">萃取法｜公式與誤差判讀總表</text>
<text x="50" y="164" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">圖中數值是自編教學資料；模型先寫條件。原題pH10.0／2.0保留，紙筆推算回C01–09。</text>
<rect x="50" y="205" width="420" height="115" rx="18" fill="#fff" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="71" y="246" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#24343f">M01｜先定問題</text>
<text x="71" y="288" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">目的與檢品</text>
<polyline points="470,262 506,262" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="506.0,262.0 494.6,268.2 494.6,255.8" fill="#61717a"/>
<rect x="510" y="205" width="420" height="115" rx="18" fill="#fff" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="531" y="246" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#24343f">M02｜準備與測量</text>
<text x="531" y="288" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">製樣、作用與條件</text>
<polyline points="930,262 966,262" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="966.0,262.0 954.6,268.2 954.6,255.8" fill="#61717a"/>
<rect x="970" y="205" width="420" height="115" rx="18" fill="#fff" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="991" y="246" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#24343f">M03｜讀訊號</text>
<text x="991" y="288" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">量、單位與處理</text>
<polyline points="1390,262 1426,262" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<polygon points="1426.0,262.0 1414.6,268.2 1414.6,255.8" fill="#61717a"/>
<rect x="1430" y="205" width="420" height="115" rx="18" fill="#fff" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1451" y="246" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#24343f">M04｜核對結論</text>
<text x="1451" y="288" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">判讀、限制與品質</text>
<rect x="65" y="365" width="559" height="324" rx="18" fill="#fff1c8" stroke="#9e771c" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="87" y="406" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#9e771c">C01／C02｜pH → 形態比例</text>
<text x="87" y="454.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">酸：A⁻/HA=10^(pH−pKa)</text>
<text x="87" y="487" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">鹼：B/BH⁺=10^(pH−pKa)</text>
<text x="87" y="519.66" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">差2→100:1，約99%是形態。</text>
<text x="87" y="552.32" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">Propranolol：10−9.5=0.5</text>
<text x="87" y="584.98" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">3²&lt;10&lt;4²→中性75–80%，約76%。</text>
<rect x="660" y="365" width="559" height="324" rx="18" fill="#d9eeea" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="682" y="406" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#286c68">C03｜單次萃取先守恆</text>
<text x="682" y="454.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">D=co/ca（總分析濃度）</text>
<text x="682" y="487" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">m0=caVa+coVo</text>
<text x="682" y="519.66" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">q=Va/(Va+DVo)，E=1−q</text>
<text x="682" y="552.32" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">Va100mL、Vo25mL、D4：</text>
<text x="682" y="584.98" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">q=1/2；60µg中萃30、剩30。</text>
<rect x="1255" y="365" width="559" height="324" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1277" y="406" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#326991">C04｜固定D的新份萃取</text>
<text x="1277" y="454.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">每份新溶劑v、共n次：</text>
<text x="1277" y="487" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">qn=[Va/(Va+Dv)]ⁿ</text>
<text x="1277" y="519.66" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">En=1−qn</text>
<text x="1277" y="552.32" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">同pH、D、Va且每次平衡；</text>
<text x="1277" y="584.98" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">搖同一份含藥溶劑，不能再乘q。</text>
<text x="70" y="784" font-size="29" font-weight="600" fill="#286c68">C05｜先由中性K求此pH的D，再算萃取比例</text>
<text x="70" y="827" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">理想弱酸：僅中性HA進有機相，單一pKa、固定pH、無離子配對或反應。下圖是模型曲線，不是實測。</text>

<circle cx="473.0" cy="1160.0" r="8" fill="#326991" stroke="#326991"/>
<polyline points="473.0,1160.0 473.0,1355" fill="none" stroke="#61717a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-dasharray="10 7"/>
<text x="493.0" y="1138" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#326991">pH4：D4</text>
<text x="91" y="936" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#326991">D</text>
<text x="758" y="1432" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">pH</text>
<text x="166" y="905" font-size="25" font-weight="600" fill="#326991">K=8、pKa=4的單一弱酸</text>
<rect x="900" y="893" width="908" height="338" rx="18" fill="#fff" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="922" y="934" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#326991">D與K不要互換</text>
<text x="922" y="982.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">K=[HA]o/[HA]a：指定中性形態</text>
<text x="922" y="1015" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">D=co/ca：指定pH的所有形態</text>
<text x="922" y="1047.66" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">D酸=K/[1+10^(pH−pKa)]</text>
<text x="922" y="1080.32" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">D鹼=K/[1+10^(pKa−pH)]</text>
<text x="922" y="1112.98" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">酸K8、pH=pKa→D4，Va100／Vo25→E1/2。</text>
<text x="922" y="1145.64" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">pH=pKa+1：D8/11，E2/13；需上述模型。</text>
<rect x="900" y="1260" width="908" height="183" rx="18" fill="#fff1c8" stroke="#9e771c" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="922" y="1301" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#9e771c">方向驗算</text>
<text x="922" y="1349.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">酸pH低→HA多→D趨K；pH高→D降。鹼方向相反。若帶電形態也能萃取，就重建各</text>
<text x="922" y="1382" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">形態守恆，不能硬用本式。</text>
<text x="70" y="1523" font-size="29" font-weight="600" fill="#286c68">C04｜同總有機溶劑50mL：一次50，與兩次各25</text>
<text x="70" y="1566" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">自編：水相100mL、初量60µg、D4。兩次分開接觸與收集；濃度比D不等於兩相總量比。</text>
<polyline points="430.0,1644 430.0,1902" fill="none" stroke="#d8dde0" stroke-width="1" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="418.0" y="1944" font-size="20" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">0</text>
<polyline points="605.0,1644 605.0,1902" fill="none" stroke="#d8dde0" stroke-width="1" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="593.0" y="1944" font-size="20" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">10</text>
<polyline points="780.0,1644 780.0,1902" fill="none" stroke="#d8dde0" stroke-width="1" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="768.0" y="1944" font-size="20" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">20</text>
<polyline points="955.0,1644 955.0,1902" fill="none" stroke="#d8dde0" stroke-width="1" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="943.0" y="1944" font-size="20" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">30</text>
<polyline points="1130.0,1644 1130.0,1902" fill="none" stroke="#d8dde0" stroke-width="1" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="1118.0" y="1944" font-size="20" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">40</text>
<polyline points="1305.0,1644 1305.0,1902" fill="none" stroke="#d8dde0" stroke-width="1" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="1293.0" y="1944" font-size="20" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">50</text>
<polyline points="1480.0,1644 1480.0,1902" fill="none" stroke="#d8dde0" stroke-width="1" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
<text x="1468.0" y="1944" font-size="20" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">60</text>
<text x="93" y="1704" font-size="26" font-weight="400" fill="#286c68">一次50mL</text>
<rect x="430" y="1659" width="700.0" height="77" rx="0" fill="#7bb6ac" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="2"/>
<rect x="1130.0" y="1659" width="350.0" height="77" rx="0" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="460" y="1706" font-size="25" font-weight="600" fill="#286c68">萃走40µg</text>
<text x="1152.0" y="1706" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#326991">剩20µg</text>
<text x="1540" y="1702" font-size="26" font-weight="400" fill="#286c68">E=2/3</text>
<text x="93" y="1824" font-size="26" font-weight="400" fill="#286c68">兩次25mL</text>
<rect x="430" y="1779" width="787.5" height="77" rx="0" fill="#7bb6ac" stroke="#286c68" stroke-width="2"/>
<rect x="1217.5" y="1779" width="262.5" height="77" rx="0" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="460" y="1826" font-size="25" font-weight="600" fill="#286c68">萃走45µg</text>
<text x="1239.5" y="1826" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#326991">剩15µg</text>
<text x="1540" y="1822" font-size="26" font-weight="400" fill="#286c68">E=3/4</text>
<text x="1310" y="1985" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">量（µg），兩色合60</text>
<text x="93" y="2012" font-size="24" font-weight="400" fill="#286c68">兩份新25mL：先萃30，再萃15，合45；水剩15。每次殘留1/2→(1/2)²；同量分次更有效。</text>
<rect x="65" y="2100" width="855" height="305" rx="18" fill="#eef6ed" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="87" y="2141" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#356844">C06｜量、回收與濃縮是三件事</text>
<text x="87" y="2189.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">m=c測×V終×DF；回收R=m終/m初</text>
<text x="87" y="2222" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">CF=c終/c初=R×V初/V終</text>
<text x="87" y="2254.66" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">20mL中40µg，收32µg於2mL：</text>
<text x="87" y="2287.32" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">R80%、終16µg/mL、CF8倍。</text>
<text x="87" y="2319.98" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">稀釋2倍測8µg/mL→16，不重複乘DF。</text>
<rect x="967" y="2100" width="855" height="305" rx="18" fill="#eef6ed" stroke="#356844" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="989" y="2141" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#356844">C07｜加標回收扣原樣</text>
<text x="989" y="2189.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">R加標=(m加標−m原)/m加入</text>
<text x="989" y="2222" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">三量先還原同份樣品與相同單位。</text>
<text x="989" y="2254.66" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">原4µg、加10µg後測12µg：</text>
<text x="989" y="2287.32" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">(12−4)/10=80%，不是120%。</text>
<text x="989" y="2319.98" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">加標反應含整條方法，不自動隔離MS基質效應。</text>
<rect x="65" y="2441" width="855" height="309" rx="18" fill="#e8e1f2" stroke="#685185" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="87" y="2482" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#685185">C08｜內標只抵消匹配損失</text>
<text x="87" y="2530.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">Ax/Ai=Fxi×mx/mi；前加且匹配</text>
<text x="87" y="2563" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">F2、內標5µg、比值4→原量10µg</text>
<text x="87" y="2595.66" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">共同60%回收→量6與3，比仍4。</text>
<text x="87" y="2628.32" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">物理回收仍60%，藥沒有找回。</text>
<text x="87" y="2660.98" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">若目標50%、內標100%，錯用簡式估5µg。</text>
<rect x="967" y="2441" width="855" height="309" rx="18" fill="#dceaf7" stroke="#326991" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="989" y="2482" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#326991">C09｜微透析RR要用分率</text>
<text x="989" y="2530.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">零目標入口、條件匹配：</text>
<text x="989" y="2563" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">RR=c透析/c游離，c游離=c透析/RR</text>
<text x="989" y="2595.66" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">透析8nM、RR40%=2/5：</text>
<text x="989" y="2628.32" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">8÷(2/5)=20nM；不是8÷40。</text>
<text x="989" y="2660.98" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">估可通膜游離成分，不是組織總量。</text>
<text x="70" y="2843" font-size="29" font-weight="600" fill="#286c68">M04｜出錯時，先辨哪種量或哪段流程</text>
<text x="70" y="2886" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">下列紅虛線是分析者回查；不是分子迴轉、流體迴流，也不是公式的運算箭頭。</text>
<rect x="65" y="2950" width="559" height="230" rx="18" fill="#fff" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="87" y="2991" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#b72d4a">分母、DF或單位錯</text>
<text x="87" y="3039.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">低濃度可能只是大體積；回收與CF、游離與總量分</text>
<text x="87" y="3072" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">開。</text>
<polyline points="343,3180 343,3218" fill="none" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-dasharray="10 7"/>
<polygon points="343.0,3218.0 336.8,3206.6 349.2,3206.6" fill="#b72d4a"/>
<text x="87" y="3250" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#b72d4a">回 M03／L24–26</text>
<rect x="660" y="2950" width="559" height="230" rx="18" fill="#fff" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="682" y="2991" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#b72d4a">真的有藥物流失</text>
<text x="682" y="3039.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">分段後測上樣／洗液／沖提與容器；查pH、材料、</text>
<text x="682" y="3072" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">降解、捕集。</text>
<polyline points="938,3180 938,3218" fill="none" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-dasharray="10 7"/>
<polygon points="938.0,3218.0 931.8,3206.6 944.2,3206.6" fill="#b72d4a"/>
<text x="682" y="3250" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#b72d4a">回 M02／L05、10、12、21</text>
<rect x="1255" y="2950" width="559" height="230" rx="18" fill="#fff" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="2"/>
<text x="1277" y="2991" font-size="27" font-weight="600" fill="#b72d4a">條件或校正失配</text>
<text x="1277" y="3039.34" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">內標時機與損失、RR流速、SPME時間與塗層；重</text>
<text x="1277" y="3072" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#24343f">核基準。</text>
<polyline points="1533,3180 1533,3218" fill="none" stroke="#b72d4a" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-dasharray="10 7"/>
<polygon points="1533.0,3218.0 1526.8,3206.6 1539.2,3206.6" fill="#b72d4a"/>
<text x="1277" y="3250" font-size="23" font-weight="400" fill="#b72d4a">回 M02–03／L22–26</text>
<text x="50" y="3362" font-size="20" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">C01–09的七階紙筆推導、量級驗算與練習在建構講義；題本沒有獨立含量計算題。本圖不是題本原始數據。</text>
<text x="50" y="3398" font-size="20" font-weight="400" fill="#61717a">原題：CH23 PDF（內題頭CH24）；正文：建構講義 v3.0。M＝大循環站；L＝理解模組；P＝方法條目。示意不按比例。</text>

## 來源範圍、改版差異與實際檢查

已讀：兩份萃取法v2.0筆記全文，與本次PDF14頁／32題／128選項。PDF檔名ch23、頁內題頭CH24，兩者原樣登記；不改成其他章題本。舊文稱31題，漏了本次第9頁109-1 #36，已補Q019。

舊筆記所引用的統整本CH23照片2頁、Watson原書與更早白話檔沒有在本次附件中，故指定Cyclizine、染料／試劑與維生素例以舊筆記來源身分保留，不宣稱重新讀到原書原圖。引用搜尋摘錄的補充文件，在S條明示可取用範圍。

主要修正：形態比例與萃取率分開；動態分配而非分子不動；中性／帶電非唯一開關；兩有機相與層位辨識；SPE流穿例外、篩板、活化與平衡；逆相有機比例；交換固定電荷與弱型pH；OH⁻競爭而非把酸中和；免疫親和可有小分子；HbA1c是血紅蛋白；RAM兼尺寸與表面；衍生化非全部不可逆；SFE收集要查回收；微透析RR與SPME非耗盡；內標前加匹配；真不可逆與題本D分界。

### S01｜IUPAC Gold Book｜distribution ratio

^ext-c-s01

[IUPAC Gold Book｜distribution ratio](https://goldbook.iupac.org/terms/view/D01817)。核對官方檢索摘錄：總分析濃度、不分化學形態的相濃度比。網頁正文取用受403限制；本版q及pH式是依本章定義與守恆作教學推導，不宣稱IUPAC原文提供這些自編數值。

### S02｜IUPAC Gold Book｜partition ratio

^ext-c-s02

[IUPAC Gold Book｜partition ratio](https://goldbook.iupac.org/terms/view/P04440)。核對檢索定義與指定形態／分配比名稱不可混用的界線；网頁正文抓取受限。本章K符號明確限定中性形態與有機／水方向，避免與D混淆。

### S03｜Waters｜SPE Method Development Summary

^ext-c-s03

[Waters｜SPE Method Development Summary](https://www.waters.com/nextgen/us/en/education/primers/beginner-s-guide-to-spe/spe-method-development-summary.html)。核對逆相提高有機含量沖提、正相與交換為不同作用及材料例。其概括表pH方向不能脫離強弱交換基照搬；正文以固定表面、藥的解離及實際產品條件判。

### S04｜Waters｜Certified Sep-Pak Care and Use，720002966en

^ext-c-s04

[Waters｜Certified Sep-Pak Care and Use，720002966en](https://help.waters.com/content/dam/waters/en/support/usermanuals/2009/720002966en/720002966en.pdf)。已讀相關步驟段落：保留目標／流穿兩模式、潤濕後平衡、穿透的上樣溶劑／容量／流速原因。不是本題本所有品牌規格，也沒有把例中體積當全產品SOP。

### S05｜Merck／Supelco｜Solid Phase Microextraction

^ext-c-s05

[Merck／Supelco｜Solid Phase Microextraction](https://www.sigmaaldrich.com/TW/en/applications/analytical-chemistry/sample-preparation/solid-phase-microextraction)。核對DI／HS、塗層吸收與吸附、GC熱脫附／LC溶劑脫附及非耗盡取樣。自編計算均不使用纖維量直接當總樣品量。

### S06｜原始研究｜微透析回收與流速／濃度，2018

^ext-c-s06

[原始研究｜微透析回收與流速／濃度，2018](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6308031/)。Recovery rates of combination antibiotic therapy using in vitro microdialysis simulating in vivo conditions。已讀方法與回收定義段，核對游離取樣、濃度梯度、膜及流速影響；此為特定體外研究，不把回收數值搬作[[Dopamine]]實測。

### S07｜原始研究｜Premier Hb9210多中心評估，2017

^ext-c-s07

[原始研究｜Premier Hb9210多中心評估，2017](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5524374/)。Multicentre evaluation of the Premier Hb9210 HbA1c analyser。核對檢索摘錄中的可逆硼酸親和cis-[[Diol]]作用；全文網頁遭驗證頁阻擋，未宣稱讀完。只作二醇機制校核，不列臨床診斷門檻。

### S08｜Merck｜LiChrospher ADS

^ext-c-s08

[Merck｜LiChrospher ADS](https://www.merckmillipore.com/Web-INTL-Site/en_US/-/USD/ShowDocument-Pronet?id=201205.027)。核對官方檢索摘錄中的親水外表面／疏水內表面與限制進入材料；全文下載連結取用逾時，未宣稱讀完。RAM細部結構依產品，圖4是原理示意。

### S09｜考選部｜112-2藥師一階藥物分析更正答案

^ext-c-s09

[考選部｜112-2藥師一階藥物分析更正答案](https://wwwq.moex.gov.tw/exam/wHandExamQandA_File.ashx?c=305&code=112100&q=1&s=22&t=M)。試題代號2305；本次核對第10題D與第37題一律給分。只確認答案與更正，表中沒有B申覆未通過的歷史說明。

可做的驗收：完整題與選項、27L↔27R、20F、13P、9C、舊block／獨有標題、雙檔连結、纸笔數值、五SVG原生XML與實際渲染、兩份MD內嵌字節一致。內部连結以静態解析核對；未在使用者Obsidian vault實開，外部化學笔記未提供，必要目標集中在導航。
